        mobileOpen: false,

        hasAnnouncementBanner: true,
        handleScroll() {
            this.navScrolled = window.scrollY > 30;
        },

            } else {
                this.hasAnnouncementBanner = false;

      document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {
        Alpine.data('searchbar', () => ({
          search(e) {
            let searchbarValue = '';
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
              searchbarValue = e.target.value;
            } else {
              searchbarValue = (e.target.nextElementSibling || e.target.previousElementSibling)
                ?.value;
            }
            const pageLangPrefix =
              document.documentElement.lang === 'en' ? '' : '/' + document.documentElement.lang;
            const pathName = window.location.pathname;
            let blogType = 'The-Monitor';
            if (pathName.includes('engineering')) blogType = 'Engineering';
            else if (pathName.includes('pup-culture')) blogType = 'Pup-Culture';
            else if (pathName.includes('ai')) blogType = 'AI';
            const blogTypeParam = pageLangPrefix === '' ? '?blog-0=' + blogType : '';
            const searchParam =
              searchbarValue ? (blogTypeParam === '' ? '?' : '&') + 's=' + searchbarValue : '';
            window.location.href = pageLangPrefix + '/blog/search/' + blogTypeParam + searchParam;
          },
        }));
      });
    </script>  </body></html>
